subproject. Update after every run (the orchestrator also writes
`output/run_manifest.json` with machine-readable timing).

**Structural-share split 2026-06-27 (DONE — full canonical regen).** Resolves the
abundance-double-count in the edge-resolution / decoupling "which arm" axis (FORMULAS §5a/§9/§11c).
Diagnosis: `c = edge_w·sm·logrpm` spends abundance twice, so `gene_share_tumor` ranks regulators
~2.4× more by abundance (within-gene ρ≈0.60 vs arm mass) than by evidence (≈0.25). Fix:
`gene_struct_share_*` = within-gene share of the **structural** contribution `edge_w·sm` (mode
`softmax`, new `config.PRESSURE_STRUCT_EXPR_MODE`), spending relative abundance **once**. New edge
columns `gene_struct_share_{gtex,nat,tumor}` + `_rank_tumor` + `_delta_tumor_gtex`; §9 quadrant
`high_share` and §14 `focal_high_regulator_share`/`competitors_carry_coupling` re-keyed onto the
structural rank (abundance kept as a separate reported axis); audit `struct_vs_abundance_resolution.tsv`.
Coupling spine `softmax_z_logrpm` and cross-state magnitude mass **unchanged** — verified: full-cohort
regen `joint_edge_class` counts match the 2026-06-26 refresh (stable 2141, acquired_unrealized 1775,
acquired_realized 640, lost 209, constitutive 127). **Full-cohort result (5,108 edges, 758
multi-regulator genes):** dominant regulator flips for **61%** of genes; within-gene abundance↔structural
rank ρ ≈ **0.17** (median) → the two axes are nearly orthogonal in crowded genes (the 120-gene smoke
understated it at 35% / ρ 0.80). Examples: ABCA1 miR-93-5p→**miR-33b-5p** (canonical regulator),
CDKN1A miR-10b-5p→miR-345-5p, CD274/PD-L1 let-7d-5p→miR-380-5p, EGFR miR-7-1-3p→miR-542-5p.
Decoupling (210 lost/nat_decoupled edges): structural `focal_high_regulator_share` 46/210,
competitors-carry-coupling 84/210, escape_scope {gene_not_repressed 101, gene_wide_derepression 63,
edge_local_escape 46}. Chain `mirna_state_class --healthy-anchor` → `decoupling_validation` →
`mir301_family_network`; log `output/logs/struct_share_regen_20260627_062913.log` (~13 min). Touches
`mirna_state_class.py`, `mir301_family_network.py`, `decoupling_validation.py`, `config.py`.

**Arm expression-detectability floor + site filter ladder 2026-06-30 (NEW canonical, method_dev).**
(a) **`arm_expression.py`** (canonical; `output/matrices/arm_expression_tiers.tsv`; config `ARM_EXPRESSED_MIN_RPM=10`,
`ARM_EXPRESSED_MIN_FRAC=0.01`): per-arm DETECTABILITY tiers (robust/conditional/silent), **max-based**: KEEP if RPM≥10 in ≥1 tumor, REMOVE
only if never reaches it. Of 789 HE-edge arms **482 expressed (220 robust + 262 conditional) / 307 silent**
(234 never-detected + 73 absent from matrix); **edges 5,219→4,628 kept (591 dropped, 11%)**. The 111
median-silent arms that spike >10 RPM in a few tumors are KEPT (context-specific induction). Silent arms can't
occupy RISC (≤2% of pool even in the most extreme tumor) or show coupling (≈0 cross-sample variance); absolute
miRNA:target stoichiometry not measurable (separate libraries). **Sensitivity** (`expressed_only=True`): acq
still wins, promiscuity harmful, weighting inert ⇒ **headline unchanged, pure noise removal**. Docs:
`method_dev/arm_expression/ARM_EXPRESSION_FLOOR.md` + concise `method_dev/arm_expression/SILENT_ARM_REMOVAL.md`. (b) **Site filter
ladder** (`method_dev/site_ladder/` — `site_filter_ladder.py`, `site_genomic_l5.py`, `validate_ladder_experimental.py`,
`SITE_FILTER_LADDER_PLAN.md`): graded miRNA-site confidence (L1 type/L2 AU+pos/L3 3′-supp/L4 conservation-PCT/
APA/L5 CLIP), **triple-validated** — experimental support rises monotonically up the ladder across ENCORI, TarBase v9
(4.72M rows; HE∩57%, LTP-functional-matched pairs 5,256), Manakov chimeric, POSTAR3 AGO (site-level top rung 86%).
(c) **Arm-locus mapping** recovered 541→773/789 (98%) via GENCODE miRNA-gene fallback (`he_edge_arm_landscape.py`,
tier-tagged). New data: `data/miRNA/Homo_sapiens_TarBase-v9.tsv.gz`, `data/external_cache/manakov_chimeric/`,
`data/external/POSTAR/`.

**Gene force-concentration / effective-N moderator 2026-06-29 (NEW descriptor, design step 1).**
First build for the aggregate-force-vs-abundance design (`method_dev/aggregate_pressure/AGGREGATE_FORCE_VS_ABUNDANCE_DESIGN.md`
§5; `[[aggregate-force-vs-abundance-design]]`). New module `gene_force_concentration.py` →
`output/coupling_predictor_comparison/gene_force_concentration.tsv` (1,408 genes / 759 multi-regulator,
5,101 edges). Per-gene contribution concentration `n_eff=(Σc)²/Σc²` + `top1_share` under base
`c=a` vs force `c=a·evidence/D(m)` (static within-universe degree), × {log abundance = max(log2RPM,0),
raw-linear RPM}. **Result — decisive for the abundance choice:** under **log** abundance the aggregate
is genuinely multi-regulator (median n_eff ≈ 2.5, **60%** of multi-reg genes have n_eff≥2; promiscuity
reorders the top regulator in **31.6%**), but under **raw-linear** it collapses to near-single-driver
(median n_eff ≈ 1.4, only **21%** n_eff≥2; reorders **8.7%**) — loading dominance ⇒ force≈abundance for
most genes, so the force-vs-base test has headroom mainly under log (or restricted to the multi-reg
tail under linear). Ex: PTEN 87 regs, n_eff log 55 vs lin 4.4, top miR-21-5p→miR-29c-5p under force.
Hill spectrum added (H0 richness / H1 exp-entropy / H2 invSimpson / H∞ 1/top1) — conclusion is
**formula-independent** (log multi-reg on all three, linear collapses on all three).

**Step 2 — force-vs-base coupling on TCGA tumor (`force_vs_base_coupling`, same module) →
`gene_force_coupling.tsv`.** Partial-ρ(predictor, expr | CPE+HRD+batch+target-CN), predictor ∈
{base=Σa, force=Σa·evidence/D(m)} × {log, raw-linear}, net repression = neg-FDR(q<0.05). **Result —
force does NOT beat abundance, even where it geometrically can.** log: base **398** net-repressed genes
vs force **374** (force *worse* by 24); in the multi-driver tail (n_eff≥2, 450 genes) base **159** vs
force **145**, Δ median ρ +0.0066 (force less repressive). raw-linear: base 399 vs force 391; tail
(186) 73 vs 70. **Correctness check built in:** for single-driver genes (n_eff<2) force ≡ base under
Spearman (scale-invariant) so Δρ=0 exactly — all action is in multi-reg genes, as the aggregation
lemma predicts, and there the promiscuity budget-split modestly *hurts* realized detection (it
downweights the high-abundance hubs that carry the coupling — consistent with MH-78/MH-44 abundance
dominance). **Takeaway:** the within-tumor *level* test does not favour force; the real differentiator,
if any, must come from the tumor↔GTEx **Δρ** (the change), a better `w_eff` (e.g. TargetScan ctx) /
genome-wide `D(m)`, or AGO-gating — not from this construction at the tumor level. Run:
`.venv/bin/python3 -m mirna_hallmark.analyses.misc.gene_force_concentration` (add `--concentration-only` to skip step 2).

**Step 3 — tumor↔GTEx Δρ (the CHANGE) 2026-06-29 (`tumor_gtex_delta_rho`) → `gene_force_delta_rho.tsv`.**
Per-gene Δρ = ρ_tumor − ρ_GTEx (partial Spearman, **symmetric composition+proliferation** confounds in
both cohorts via `cross_state_coupling._state_covariates` — tumor-only purity/HRD/CN have no GTEx analog),
for {base=Σa, validated-α0 force=Σ a·log1p(ev)}. Cohorts: TCGA tumor vs **GTEx breast (327 paired)**; ±
sets = curated `annotations/breast_oncomir_tsg_positive_set.tsv` (18 scored TSGs) vs 648 miRNA-poor genes.
**RESULT — the Δρ axis is REAL and biologically validated, but force still ties abundance.** Positive set:
median ρ_tumor **−0.097** vs ρ_GTEx **−0.016** ⇒ Δρ **−0.082** (known oncomiR-TSGs ACQUIRE repression in
tumor; ~none in healthy); negatives Δρ ≈ −0.007. Δρ **significantly separates positives from miRNA-poor
genes: Mann-Whitney p=0.0028 (base) / 0.0021 (force_val_a0)** — the validation positive control works.
Force is a hair better (Δρ −0.086 vs −0.082, sep −0.079 vs −0.075) but **not meaningfully — a tie**, like
every other rung. **Arc verdict:** abundance-sum is the right gene-rung dose on the *level* AND the
*change*; binding-weight/promiscuity add nothing. The valuable finding is the **reframe** (level→change):
the tumor-vs-healthy CHANGE in aggregate miRNA coupling is detectable and lands on the right (curated) genes.
Run: `.venv/bin/python3 -m mirna_hallmark.analyses.misc.gene_force_concentration --delta-rho`. Caveat: Δρ conditions on
composition+proliferation (symmetric), not target-CN (no normal analog); positive n=18 (small, effect clear).
**Added predictor `acq` = rectified ACQUIRED abundance `max(a−h,0)`** (h=`healthy_anchor.gtex_qn_baseline`,
QN healthy baseline on TCGA scale; nonlinear, so not the inert centering no-op). **FIRST construction to
move magnitude:** positive-set ρ_tumor **−0.124** (vs abundance −0.097, ~28%), Δρ **−0.114** (vs −0.082),
pos-vs-neg separation **−0.104** (vs −0.075) — ~30–40% bigger effect; but significance flat (MWU p 0.0022
vs 0.0028, rectification adds variance). GTEx-side rectification is ill-posed (healthy vs own baseline) →
`ρ_tumor` is the cleaner metric. Lead: pursue `acq` via ρ_tumor, recover power, test acq×evidence. See
design §4b.

**Step 3b FINAL — full paired head-to-head by ρ_tumor (`tumor_acquired_coupling`, `--acq-tumor`) →
`gene_acquired_tumor_coupling.tsv` 2026-06-29.** Canonical confounds **+proliferation** (MH-78 gate);
8 constructions {base, val_a0/a1, ts_a0/a1, acq, acq_ev, acq_ts} scored by ρ_tumor; **each paired per-gene
vs base** (Wilcoxon + bootstrap 95%CI) on positives(18)/orthogonal-TSG(44)/genome-wide. **DEFINITIVE
verdict:** (1) **edge weighting INERT** — evidence `val_a0` positives p=0.051 (CI touches 0), TargetScan
`ts_a0` p=0.64, genome-wide medΔ≈0 (the earlier "weighting beats base with prolif" was n=18-median NOISE,
now refuted); (2) **promiscuity HARMFUL** — `val_a1` medΔ +0.038 (p=0.99), `ts_a1` +0.030; (3) **acq & acq_ev
the ONLY robust wins** — positives p=8e-4/7e-4, TSG p=5e-4/2e-4, genome-wide p=1.3e-14/2.9e-13, all bootstrap
CI exclude 0; survives proliferation; **acq_ev≈acq ⇒ acquired transform is the whole lever, weight adds
nothing**; `acq_ts` not robust (TS 320-arm coverage). Mechanism: coupling=cross-SAMPLE correlation, weights
are cross-REGULATOR constants (inert, loading-dominance pins them), acq reshapes per-arm sample trajectory.
**Consolidated readable findings doc written: `method_dev/aggregate_pressure/AGGREGATE_PRESSURE_FINDINGS.md`** (Q1–Q6, rationale,
tests, results, reproduce, write-up framing). Run: `.venv/bin/python3 -m mirna_hallmark.analyses.misc.gene_force_concentration --acq-tumor`.

**Canonical-budget fix + rerun 2026-06-29.** `_gw_validated_budget` was using the build_edges DEFAULT
scorer (study-class counts) for `D_val`, not the spine's canonical `confidence_logclass`+ENCORI (config
`PRESSURE_EVIDENCE_SCORER`); fixed to replicate `load_mirtar_edges` genome-wide (confidence_logclass,
score≥`PRESSURE_MIN_EVIDENCE`=2, +ENCORI α=0.5). Rebuilt budgets (validated 1,045→**1,036 arms**, median
D 3.89→**3.55**, miR-21-5p still top) + reran the head-to-head. **Verdict unchanged/confirmed:** promiscuity
still harmful (`val_a1` paired Δ +0.038 p=0.99, `ts_a1` +0.030), weighting inert, `acq`/`acq_ev` the only
wins (currency fix moved val_a1 by 0.0007). Also corrected: the analysis edge set is **confidence_logclass≥2
+ENCORI (5,219 edges/1,424 genes; 5,101/1,408 expressible)**, NOT the older HE mask; findings doc updated
(canonical evidence def, exact counts, n_eff=cohort-median not per-sample mean, symmetric composition defined,
proliferation canonical).

**Step 2 REBUILT with corrected (canonical) construction 2026-06-29 — the strawman D(m) above
was within-universe degree; replaced.** New canonical module `genome_wide_promiscuity.py` →
`output/matrices/gw_budget_{validated,targetscan}.tsv`: `D(m)` = arm's **genome-wide binding BUDGET**
(evidence-mass, NOT degree) = Σ over the arm's whole targetome of the per-edge weight, **HE edges only**
for validated. `W(m,g)=w_eff/D^α` is then the fraction of m's budget on g (true budget-split). Budget
rankings confirm the bias contrast: validated top = **miR-21-5p** (most-studied), TargetScan top =
**miR-15/16/195** (biggest seed targetome). `gene_force_concentration.py` rewired: two currencies
(validated log1p(evidence) / TargetScan |context++|) × α∈{0,0.5,1} × {log,lin}, scored partial-ρ
(CPE+HRD+batch+target-CN). **FAIR result (matched gene sets, Δmedian ρ ≈ 0.0000 throughout) — NO force
construction beats abundance-sum, and promiscuity penalty monotonically HURTS:** base_log **398**
net-repressed; validated force α0 **397** (tie), α0.5 386, α1 **367** (worse); TargetScan force (777-gene
coverage — only 320 arms have a TS budget) on matched genes base 277 → α0 **278** (tie), α1 **267**.
TargetScan is **not** stronger than validated (both tie abundance at α=0, both degrade with α). So the
fair test confirms MH-78/MH-44: **abundance-sum is the right gene-rung dose**; a real binding model
(evidence OR TargetScan context++) adds nothing at the tumor level, and ÷D over-penalizes the
high-abundance hubs that carry coupling (supports catalytic/sublinear promiscuity, α→0). Only remaining
live test = tumor↔GTEx **Δρ** (the change). Run: `.venv/bin/python3 -m mirna_hallmark.analyses.misc.genome_wide_promiscuity`
(build budgets) then `-m mirna_hallmark.analyses.misc.gene_force_concentration`.
**Phase 0 (honest record) 2026-06-27:** persisted `struct_vs_abundance_coupling_assessment.tsv`
(`_struct_coupling_assessment`, regenerated each state_class run) proving **share ⊥ coupling**
(within-gene ρ(share,−adj_rho)≈0; #1-pick FDR-hit abundance 34.7% > structural 27.0% = power
artifact); added the share⊥coupling CAVEAT to FORMULAS §5a/§9 (high struct share ≠ repression).
**Phase 2 grounding 2026-06-27:** evidence-scoring (`confidence_logclass`) + ENCORI-mRNA boost
(`PRESSURE_ENCORI_ALPHA=0.5`) already live ⇒ option D/D2 DONE; within-gene `edge_w` spread now
median **2.44×** (was ~1.8×). Remaining lever = TargetScan-affinity resolver (options A/E), but
`ts_weight` is **not** merged into M0 edges. **Reframe:** baseline hub rank-ρ(edge_w, realized
|contrib|) = **−0.044 median** (PTEN +0.16 … CDKN1A −0.21) — realized magnitude is abundance-led
by construction, so the old PRESSURE_FUTURE_OPTIONS gate (edge_w rank-ρ>0.25 vs abundance-contrib)
is self-defeating; Phase 2 should be gated on **coupling** (Basal ≥42/50 key 8/8) + structural-
attribution validity instead. Affinity-resolver build + grid PENDING (greenlight needed before
spine-candidate).
**Phase 1 decoupling estimator — Part B increment 2026-06-27 (DONE).** Math first settled the
*predictor* (Part A): a partial **Spearman** is rank-based, so `edge_w` is rank-invisible (constant
per edge) and `z`/abundance/`logRPM` are mutually rank-identical (`coupling_predictor_comparison`) —
the **softmax share is the only univariate term that changes a per-edge rank**, and it is just a
nonlinear rank-1 proxy for co-regulator competition. So competition was moved into the **estimator**
as a covariate. Added to `decoupling_validation` (block 6 + anchor): **competition control** —
condition the tumour partial on the gene's competitive field `P_others=Σ_{m'≠m} c(m',g,s)`
(`compute_edge_pressure_map`, softmax_logrpm); `competition_masked` (re-emerges → co-regulator hid m,
not a true loss) / `competition_explained` (residual was the field). Result on the 210 lost/nat_decoupled
edges: **1 masked / 1 explained, median Δ≈0 → losses robust to competition (genuine)**; tail cases
miR-1246→NFIB (−0.29→−0.40 masked), miR-221-3p→DVL2 (−0.07→+0.04 explained); n_compet median 909.
**GTEx-primary anchor** (`healthy_anchor_class`, replacing the permissive `g_neg OR n_neg` for
interpretation): **123 gtex_anchored / 13 nat_only (field-effect-suspect) / 74 neither** (pressure-
trajectory losses, not coupling-anchored). FORMULAS §14 updated.
**Phase 1 PERFORMANCE evaluation 2026-06-27 (`evaluate()`, `--evaluate`, n_boot=n_perm=200, ~6 min):**
bootstrap CI + permutation null + loss-call stability → `decoupling_validation_eval.tsv` +
`eval_method_manifest.json`. **How the new modeling performs:** (1) competition masking is
**well-calibrated** — observed **0 bootstrap-robust** masked vs **0.24 expected under the P_others
permutation null** (the lone point-estimate flag, miR-1246→NFIB, does NOT survive bootstrap); (2) the
competitive field carries **real but negligible** info — **106/210 (50%) edges perm-significant**
(median perm-p 0.04) but **median |Δ|=0.008, ~13× below the 0.10 masking bar** (CI width 0.038); (3)
**loss calls are only ~80% stable** under patient resampling ⇒ **edge-level decoupling is low-SNR**.
Net: the competition+GTEx additions are **validation/confidence layers, not relabelers** (competition
reclassifies ~0, GTEx flags 13 nat_only). **Per-edge rigor COMPLETED** — extended bootstrap CI +
robustness uniformly to CN/meth/ATAC (`_boot_condition`): **0 bootstrap-robust masks of ANY kind**
(competition/CN/meth/ATAC all 0; the point-estimate cnv 1/meth 2/atac 1/compet 1 flags do NOT survive
resampling) ⇒ **the per-edge masking layer is entirely point-estimate noise** — the modeling correctly
reports the edge level can't support these calls. **Go-up reframe (measured):** aggregating
edge→arm-module→gene does **NOT** magnify effect size (median strong-subset ρ_gtex ≈ −0.15 at *every*
level; healthy repression is weak everywhere) — so going up is a **stability** play (composite denoises
the target), not a signal-magnification one. Also aligned block-6 base/comp samples. **Deferred:**
formal per-sample mediation = arm-level by nature; GTEx-primary into `joint_edge_class` = gated
spine-overwrite (separate decision). Touches `decoupling_validation.py`.
**GO-UP test 2026-06-27 (`--go-up` → `decoupling_go_up_stability.tsv`) — hypothesis REFUTED.** Tested
whether the healthy(GTEx) repression call is more bootstrap-stable at the arm-module level than per
edge. **Opposite:** edge healthy-repressive-call stability median **95%** (76% stable≥0.9) vs arm-module
**89%** (45%). Paired per-arm (same 54 arms, removes pre-selection confound): **best single edge
ρ_gtex −0.209 vs arm-module −0.074 (3× weaker), module dilutes in 93% of arms; best-edge stability 97%
vs module 32%.** ⇒ **the realized repression signal is CONCENTRATED at specific functional edges, not
diffuse across the module; aggregating up DILUTES it** (pools the arm's 1–few real targets with its many
non-functional ones). **Conclusion of the decoupling arc:** the within-state repression call is stable
at the EDGE level (95–97%); the genuinely low-SNR part is only the cross-state *transition* (tumor-flat
~80%, tiny Δ, 0 robust masks) — a biology/data limit (the healthy→tumor coupling change is small), NOT a
resolution problem. **Right direction = functional-edge-selected reads (where signal concentrates), not
module aggregation and not noisy all-edge.** FORMULAS §14 updated.
**Functional-edge selection 2026-06-27 (`--functional-edges` → `functional_edge_decoupling.tsv`).**
Of 210 'lost' edges, **100 have strong+stable healthy repression** (ρ_gtex≤−0.15, GTEx-call stability
≥90%); of those **70 are GOLD decoupling** (also genuinely+stably flat in tumour). ⇒ the trustworthy
decoupling set is **70/210 (33%)**; the other 67% are point-estimate 'losses' the data can't support
(e.g. gold: miR-205→LMNA, miR-128→BAX, miR-218→LEF1, miR-24→MMP14, miR-145→IGF1).
**No-softmax competition check (parallelism caveat).** The block-6 competition field `P_others` is
built from softmax-`c`, and the softmax is itself a gene-local competition normalization — so the two
are parallel/entangled. Reran the competition conditioning with a **raw** `P_others` (Σ other
regulators' raw log2 abundance, NO softmax, NO edge_w): the two Δ's are **uncorrelated (−0.15)** and
raw shows more movement (|Δ|>0.05 in 23% vs 11% — the softmax version is damped by sharing the focal
edge's denominator), but **both give median Δ≈0 and masked≈1** ⇒ the not-competition-confounded
verdict is **representation-independent**. (Raw-`P_others` is the cleaner control; wiring it as a
persisted parallel column is a small TODO.)
**GENERAL edge-conclusion robustness 2026-06-27 (NOT just decoupling).** Bootstrap (NB=150) tumour
coupling-call stability across a stratified 50/class sample of ALL joint_edge_classes — the first
resampling check of the framework's *confident* classes, not only the lost subset. **Confident classes
are ROBUST:** `acquired_realized` (the headline "gained realized repression", 640 edges) 96% repressive,
median ρ −0.184, **99% call-stability**; `constitutive` 90% repressive, ρ −0.150, **95% stability**.
**The decoupling low-SNR is LOCALIZED, not systemic:** instability sits only in the *borderline* classes
(`lost` call-stability 57%, `acquired_unrealized` 59%) which are DEFINED by ρ≈0 (correct behavior, not a
defect — they're stably flat). ⇒ the per-edge low-SNR found in the decoupling arc does **not** undermine
the framework's main conclusions. Minor caveat: ~31% of even `acquired_realized` edges fall below 90%
call-stability (median 99%, but a less-stable tail exists — flag in per-edge claims). Stratified sample,
not exhaustive. Script: `scratchpad/gen_stab.py`.

Fixed to a SCALE-FREE relative floor (`min_sd_rel=0.25` × the cohort's own median sd); the gene set grows **137 → 224** (edges ~1200 → ~2000). **RE-RUN: the conclusion is UNCHANGED and the numbers barely move** — GTEx a 0.112→**0.109**, ρ 0.169→**0.156**, τ 0.0008→**0.0008**, info 2.4%→**2.4%** · CPTAC a 0.336→**0.335**, τ→**0.0023**, info 0.6%→**0.8%** · NAT a 0.327→**0.334**, τ 0.0268→**0.0238**, info 0.4%→**0.7%**. τ is still indistinguishable from zero and info is still ~1% ⇒ **the state channel stays CANCELLED.** ⚠ **THIRD CORRECTION:** the SHUFFLE-NULL verdict for NAT and CPTAC is **BORDERLINE and FLIPS with the gene set** (NAT's null was 'not dead' on the old set, 'dead' on the corrected one; CPTAC the reverse). GTEx's null is dead on both. ⇒ **the earlier claim that 'NAT's shuffle-null fails' is RETRACTED — it is unstable, not a finding.**  Under a **calibrated** gauge (split-half control a=1.125 [1.00,1.28], cal✓) and the **E3 standardized-β convention**: **GTEx a=0.112 ρ=0.169 τ=0.0008 info=2.4% (null dead) · CPTAC-mRNA a=0.336 ρ=0.313 τ=0.0024 info=0.6% (null dead) · NAT a=0.327 ρ=0.287 τ=0.0268 info=0.4% (⚠ NULL NOT DEAD, a_null=0.076)**. **(1)** τ — the per-edge acquired/lost-wiring payload, i.e. the axis's ENTIRE deliverable — is indistinguishable from zero. **(2)** the coupling contribution is **0.4–2.4%** of the mRNA likelihood's own precision (a same-cohort half gives 124%); structurally, precision ∝ a² and a≈0.11 ⇒ even infinite GTEx donors add ~1%. **(3)** the handoff's additive `Δ = M_T − M_H` would have MANUFACTURED the headline (β_H ≈ 0.11·β_T ⇒ Δ>0 on nearly every edge). **(4) TWO GAUGE PATHOLOGIES CAUGHT BY THE CONTROLS (both about the GAUGE, not the model):** Fed the **Gibbs posterior's** β/SD the split-half control returns **a=4.1** (3.5e6 in one config) where the truth is 1.0. ⚠ **CORRECTED: this is NOT a defect in Gibbs — it is the BETTER estimator (split-half reproducibility ρ=0.822 vs bagged NNLS's 0.729) and the MODEL KEEPS IT.** The fault is *this gauge's* errors-in-variables correction, which divides by `Var(β̂)−mean(se²)`; Gibbs's `mean(se²)` is dominated by a **heavy tail of a few enormous posterior SDs** (`sqrt(mean(se²))=0.055` vs a typical se of 0.015) ⇒ reliability **0.17** vs NNLS's **0.72**. `_MIN_RELIABILITY` + `Gauge.usable` now REFUSE the gauge instead of returning 4.1. **Bagged NNLS for the GAUGE; Gibbs for the MODEL.** And the **raw-`r` convention silently absorbed the Y-scale** (median sd(resid Y): TCGA 0.237 vs NAT 0.600 / GTEx 0.612 ⇒ a_raw/a_zY = 2.25 ≈ the sd ratio 2.58), so the earlier "healthy β is 23% of tumour = composition attenuation" was mostly Y-SCALE. Fixed by E3 (`zscore_y=True`) + `Gauge.usable`, which now REFUSES to emit channel terms until `calibrate()` passes. **(5)** the one estimator-robust quantity is ρ: split-half 0.72 (ceiling) · NAT 0.27–0.39 · GTEx 0.10–0.18. **⇒ `channel_state.py` NOT BUILT; board §C CLOSED.** ⚠ **Hits the PROTEIN channel too** — CPTAC-mRNA is the easiest possible cross-cohort transfer and caps at 0.6%. | `learned/confounders.py` (`build_C` — ONE confounder block for FOUR cohorts) | 2026-07-12 | ok (built; **additive — nothing rewired yet**) | <1 min | **MH-102 (a) — the shared C both cross-cohort channels depend on.** C was built in **three incompatible places**: `data.assemble_gene` (TCGA: CPE + target_cn + mal_prolif + optional deconv), `states.{_cibersortx_state_cov,_state_metagene_cov}` (NAT from the pooled file; **GTEx a metagene FALLBACK**), `eval/cptac_validation._covariates` (CPTAC: ESTIMATE purity + CIN, **NO composition at all**). Both channels under construction are CROSS-COHORT (state GTEx→TCGA, protein CPTAC→TCGA), and a channel's `pihat` is commensurate with the model's β only if both β were defined after removing the same kind of variance ⇒ three builders made that impossible. **`build_C(cohort, participants)` + `availability()`** (the contract) unify TCGA · NAT · GTEx · **CPTAC**, all on Wu-9 (same 9 cell-type semantics). **VERIFIED DROP-IN:** composition ≡ `data._deconv()` (max\|diff\|=**0.0**, 1059 participants, `_DECONV_COLS` column order) AND ≡ `states._cibersortx_state_cov()` (max\|diff\|=**0.0**, 113) ⇒ **migrating TCGA and NAT is a NO-OP**; only GTEx (had no C) and CPTAC (had no composition) change. **Purity stays COHORT-NATIVE and is deliberately NOT harmonized** (TCGA `CPE` · CPTAC `ESTIMATE` · NAT/GTEx **none**) — measured benign (below). **⚠ NAT/GTEx must have NO purity term:** the clinical table is *participant*-keyed, so a naive join silently hands **107/113** NAT samples their own patient's **TUMOUR** CPE (a covariate describing a different specimen); `build_C` returns `None` to block it. NAT's `Cancer Epithelial` is **misassignment, not occult tumour** (r=−0.157 with the patient's tumour CPE, but **+0.414 with NAT's own `Normal Epithelial`**). **Adipose metagene DEFAULT-OFF** (over-control risk; see registry). Outputs: code only. Docs: registry MH-102, `LEARNED_MODEL_STATE_CHANNEL_PLAN.md` §4, `data/external/cibersortx/README.md`, DATA_SOURCES §1. |
| `learned/gauge.py` (cross-cohort GAUGE `a` + the shared SHUFFLE-NULL primitive) | 2026-07-12 | ok (built + validated; **2 estimator bugs caught by the null**) | ~3 min/source (150 genes) | **MH-102 (b) — `β_source = a·β_target + c + δ`, the scale every cross-cohort channel needs.** A channel whose evidence lives in another cohort cannot feed raw `β_source` in as `pihat`: bulk composition dilutes β differently (GTEx ~32% epithelial vs an epithelial-dominant tumour), platforms differ, and the C blocks **cannot** be column-identical (`CPE`/`target_cn` don't exist in healthy tissue). Under the naive additive `Δ = β_T − β_H` that attenuation alone makes **Δ>0 on nearly every edge** ⇒ it MANUFACTURES "acquired wiring" from an artifact. `fit_gauge` estimates ONE global `a` (errors-in-variables, attenuation-corrected slope; fit on **ALL** edges — conditioning on both-nonzero selects for agreement and biases `a` up); `a_for()` returns it **OUT-OF-FOLD over genes** so the channel never uses a scale calibrated on its own gene; `to_channel_terms()` emits the `channels=` list (`pihat=(β̂_src−c)/a`, `s²=(τ²+max(se,se_min)²)/a²`, `members=[(col,1.0)]`) ⇒ Gaussian-conjugate, **stays J1/Gibbs**. Also ships `beta_table(cohort)` + `cohort_matrices(cohort)` — the shared β producer both channels use. **THE CALIBRATION LADDER (150 genes, all three PASS their shuffled null):** CPTAC-mRNA→TCGA **a=2.369** [1.55,3.38] ρ=0.348 · NAT→TCGA **a=0.528** [0.32,0.87] ρ=0.283 · GTEx→TCGA **a=0.231** [0.09,0.38] ρ=0.168. **It FACTORIZES: state ≈2× attenuation (NAT, same patients+platform) × platform/cohort ≈2.3× (GTEx/NAT) ≈ 4.3 ≈ 1/0.231** — the plan's P0e platform-vs-state separation, answered. **⚠ HEADLINE FOR THE PROTEIN CHANNEL: a=2.37, not 1** — even a *same-modality, same-disease-state* cross-cohort transfer needs a 2.4× rescale ⇒ CPTAC **cannot** feed β_protein in raw. **TWO ESTIMATOR BUGS CAUGHT BY THE FALSIFICATION TEST (neither by reasoning):** (1) the first implementation used **TLS through the origin** → the SHUFFLED NULL DID NOT COLLAPSE (a_null=0.254 [0.19,0.34] vs a_real=0.504) because **bagged NNLS β is ≥0 with a positive mean even on pure noise**, so an origin-forced slope picks up `E[x]·E[y]>0` regardless of covariation (the shuffled *Spearman* was correctly dead at 0.084 — that discrepancy exposed it); replaced by the centered EIV slope + intercept `c` (which absorbs the NNLS positivity floor) ⇒ **null now dead** (a_null=0.054 [−0.03,0.14], 4.3× separation). (2) an NNLS coefficient pinned at the boundary β̂=0 in every bootstrap gets **se=0** — a boundary artifact, not precision — so with the intercept it produced `pihat=(0−c)/a<0` at **z≈−6**: the channel would assert, confidently, that β is NEGATIVE, on **42% of source edges**, crushing real edges under the half-normal gauge. Fixed with `se_floor()` (median positive se) ⇒ z −6 → −0.72; across all 759 terms z∈[−0.74,+4.69], only 5 with \|z\|>3. `_TAU_FLOOR_FRAC=0.15` floors τ — this is where the **C-construction/reference nuisance** lives (the measured CPTAC-on-REF-B term, β ρ=0.986), so it is not mistaken for precision. `shuffled_gauge()` = the shared cross-cohort SHUFFLE-NULL (permute the source cohort's sample labels → refit β → `a` must collapse), the analogue of `exclusion(shuffle_cn=)`, reused by both channels. Docs: registry MH-102, plan §2/§5, CHANNEL_FUSION §E/§I2. |
| `learned/immune_completeness.py` (is C's IMMUNE control sufficient?) | 2026-07-12 | ok (verdict: **incomplete but INERT — C unchanged**) | ~4 min | **MH-102 (c) — the one open C question, closed.** Panel-invariance (β ρ=0.94 across atlases) says C's *span* is STABLE; it does **not** say it is SUFFICIENT — and Wu-9's lymphoid tracks Thorsson's infiltration score at only **r=0.31** where **LM22 reaches 0.70**, while Wu's `T-cells` is the ONE technically-unreliable column (reference-resample r=0.685/0.731 vs ≥0.89 for the rest). Tested on the MH-100 pattern (residual → lever → over-control guard), in tumour, against two yardsticks C never sees (**LM22** 22-type immune reference, 1106 tumours; **Thorsson**'s published immune scores). **(1) RESIDUAL — C IS immune-incomplete:** variance surviving C = LM22-lymphoid **0.505** · TH_ifng_response **0.763** · TH_wound_healing 0.523 · metagene 0.284 · TH_lymphocyte_infiltration 0.218. (2+3) **LEVER + OVER-CONTROL — but the fix is INERT:** adding a structural lymphocyte marker metagene (the only *cross-cohort-computable* candidate — LM22/Thorsson are TCGA-only and can never be covariates) moves β by **ρ=0.967** (0.961 nonzero) with mean\|β\| 0.0072→0.0068 (−5%), and the shift is the same on immune target genes (ρ=0.959, 44 genes) as non-immune (ρ=0.973, 75) ⇒ neither de-confounding nor over-controlling. **VERDICT: C UNCHANGED.** Contrast with MH-100, where the analogous residual crack WAS a lever (β −20–30%); here the crack is real but immaterial — the program's recurring "immaterial-at-n≈1000". Caveat: this tests the **mRNA-coupling** β; protein discordance may be more composition-sensitive, and that cannot be tested until the protein channel exists. Docs: registry MH-102, plan §4.3. |

**no mouse leakage** (all `hsa-`; 1 cosmetic `miR-21a`). **Downstream: (a) + (b) BUILT (2026-07-11); (c) OPEN.** **(a) ✅ BUILT — relatedness-gated host conditioning IN the CN exclusion (`host_pleiotropy`, wired into `exclusion(host=True)`, see the dedicated MH-101 row below):** host FOLDED INTO `coding_pleiotropy` as a guard-exempt candidate → ONE JOINT `pleio_down_weight` (retired MAX; de-double-counts collinear confounders). Full-universe joint batch (`coding=True, host=True`, optimized 11→2.3 s/gene, ~24 min) down-weights **48 well-instrumented segments (37 host · 11 pure-coding; 11 clean · 37 repression_direction)** — IL6@CTDSP2* 0.87, PIK3CG@HNRNPK* 0.67, CCNE1@SMC4* 0.51, ESR1@CTDSP2* 0.50, MCM7 cluster guard-exempt; gate partials the miRNA out + δ_s-sign confidence (user upgrades); ⚠ multi-locus host caveat (safe no-op direction). **(b) ✅ BUILT — the generalized HOST-GENE coupling-confounder lens (`host_confound_lens`) = MH-100 generalized:** `prolif_verdict`'s OOF 2×2 but conditioning on the SPECIFIC host gene (added IN ADDITION to `mal_prolif`, orthogonalized ⇒ tests the host confound BEYOND proliferation). **RESULT (403 related coding-host edges under the miR-partial gate, repression-directed −ρ): confound 77 · over_control 130 · fragile 32 · neutral 164; mean dC −0.032** — and **60/77 confound edges (78%) are NOVEL non-proliferation hosts** (miR-186→FOXO1, miR-7→IGF1R; robust to CPE+prolif; NO direct OmniPath/CollecTRI H→T edge ⇒ shared-upstream co-regulation) invisible to MH-100's prolif-only lens ⇒ the host gene is a DIFFERENT confound axis; gene-specific FLAG (`host_prolif_corr`/`host_novel`, `output/learned/host_confound_lens.tsv`), not a global lever. **⚠ user-caught abs()→−ρ fix (2026-07-11):** the OOF coupling used |ρ| (conflated repression-strengthening with sign-FLIPS to anti-repression, e.g. miR-23b→PLAU −0.04→+0.16); | `instrument.host_pleiotropy` + `exclusion(host=)`/`run_exclusion(host=,workers=)` (intronic-HOST CN-exclusion gate = MH-101 downstream (a)) | 2026-07-11 | ok (built + full-universe batch run) | 22 min (8-worker BLAS-pinned) | **MH-101 (a) — the CN-exclusion wiring of the genomic-context axis (design axis BP).** A SENSE-intronic miRNA's locus CN co-amplifies its HOST gene by construction (same locus, perfect co-amp); if the host RELATES to the target the host-protein path Z_s→CN_host→Y bypasses the miRNA and violates exclusion. **`host_pleiotropy(gene, mem)`** = the a-priori extreme of `coding_pleiotropy` (MH-99): host from ANNOTATION (`genomic_context`, sense_coding_host), gated by the validated `host_target_relatedness` (partial ρ(host,target\|CPE,**miRNA**)≥0.3 — the miRNA partialled OUT, see below), same `condition-δ_s` + **ACT-ONLY-ON-REDUCTIONS** core; emits `host_down_weight`∈[0,1] that **inflates s²_π** (weaker CN pull, NOT a subtraction). Wired into **`exclusion(coding=True, host=True)`** as ONE JOINT gate (host folded into `coding_pleiotropy` as a MANDATORY GUARD-EXEMPT candidate via `host_genes=`/`scan=`; conditions {coding ∪ host} JOINTLY → one `pleio_down_weight`, RETIRING the earlier **MAX** heuristic — MAX assumed same-slice, but co-amplified confounders are collinear so the joint **de-double-counts**: ESR1 0.50 vs MAX 0.72, PIK3CG 0.67 vs 0.90). Cols `pleio_down_weight`/`s2_pi_pleio`/`beta_weight_pleio`/`pleio_confidence`/`pleio_sources` (host tagged `*`). **COMPLEMENTARY, not redundant (measured on 29 scoped host-confounds):** the generic window scan MISSES **15/29** and lists the host itself in only 5 — starkest for proliferation hosts (MCM7 for miR-17~92/106b~25) that the scan's prolif-survival guard drops but the host gate retains (⚠ for those the flag overlaps MH-100's confound-vs-mechanism → cross-reference `prolif_verdict`, don't blind-apply). **THREE USER-DRIVEN UPGRADES (2026-07-11): (3) the JOINT gate above (retired MAX);** (1) **miRNA partialled OUT of the relatedness gate** — it must isolate the host's OWN target path, not the host proxying the miR's transcription (co-transcription ρ≈0.29); empirically does NOT lose host signal (mean\|ρ\| 0.184→0.190, net MORE inclusive 372→403 related) but reshuffles — demotes co-transcription-entangled MCM7 edges, reveals miR-masked ones. (2) **δ_s-sign CONFIDENCE flag** — a residual-miR leak has sign −sign(γ), so δ_s SAME-sign as γ can't be lost-miR ⇒ `clean_antirepression` (unambiguous); opposite ⇒ `repression_direction` (needs the miR-partial + prolif x-ref). **BATCH (`run_exclusion(coding=True, host=True)`, full HE universe, 8-worker BLAS-pinned + OPTIMIZED loop — precomputed lstsq projectors replace per-gene sklearn fits, vectorised co-amp, window scan restricted to host families ⇒ 11→2.3 s/gene, ~24 min):** base table **byte-identical** (45,156 / 17,040 / 4,342 / 12,698); **48 well-instrumented (F>10) segments down-weighted — 37 host-involving · 11 pure-coding; 11 clean · 37 repression_direction**: IL6/miR-26a@CTDSP2* 0.87, IGF1/miR-28@LPP* 0.84, HMGCS2/miR-425@DALRD3* 0.72, PIK3CG/miR-7@HNRNPK* 0.67, CCNE1/miR-16@SMC4* 0.51, ESR1/miR-26a@CTDSP2* 0.50, FBP1/miR-21-3p@VMP1* 0.43, MCM7 cluster (guard-exempt `MCM7*` alone) 0.30–0.36. **⚠ MULTI-LOCUS — instrument side RESOLVED, host-annotation residual:** `exclusion` now DECOUPLES X_fam (per-arm) from instrument columns (per **active-source locus** via `_arm_active_loci`) — but silent-source loci aren't valid instruments, so focal-per-arm was EXACT for **783/785** arms; only miR-550a-3p/5p (≥2 active sources) change (now multi_iv-complete + within-arm over-ID). ESR1 etc. byte-identical. RESIDUAL = host-annotation granularity: host is per-arm→focal locus; 7/141 context-mixed multi-locus related arms (miR-16@SMC4-vs-DLEU2) may have focal≠coding-host copy — but a non-co-amplified host is a NO-OP (dw≈0, can't false-down-weight; non-zero dw self-validates), cost = under-detection; fix = per-locus host annotation via MI↔GENCODE map (→ (c) multi-arm) **[✅ RESOLVED 2026-07-11 — see the PER-LOCUS row below; the base is now 45,187 after the `.N` universe refresh, not 45,156]**. Earlier gate-align: `coding_pleiotropy` candidate filter → partial **Spearman**\|C ≥0.3. **Coupling consumer `host_confound_lens` re-run on the new gate: 403 edges, confound 77 (60/77=78% novel non-prolif), over_control 130, fragile 32, neutral 164, mean dC −0.032** (headline stable; also folded the novel-host flag INTO the function for reproducibility). The design's clean alternative (AL2 intronic/pre-mRNA nascent-transcription latent, which would cleanly separate transcription-rate from proliferation-state) is **DATA-BLOCKED** — no intronic reads in the repo. Deliverable = the per-gene classifier as a **flag, not a subtraction** (sibling of MH-99's "trust only reductions"). (b) **PER-EDGE (`prolif_verdict_edges_genomewide.tsv`, 1242 coupled):** mechanism 472 (38%) · ambiguous 328 (26%) · **prolif_robust 245 (20%)** · **deconfound 106 (8.5%)** · **unmasked_real 91 (7.3%)** — controlling proliferation would over-strip mediated signal (38%) far more than remove genuine confound (8.5%); ~7% of edges are actively HIDDEN by proliferation (`unmasked_real` = discovery leads); 20% proliferation-robust. (c) **ARM-SOURCE (#2, `arm_prolif_source`/`arm_source_scan` → `prolif_arm_source.tsv`):** a family's proliferation confound is NOT diffuse — it localizes to a SINGLE arm (**median concentration 0.85**), systematically the abundant amplicon-resident arm: **miR-93-5p** (106b~25 cluster, 84–95% dose × ρ_prolif +0.42) carries the miR-17~92 confound across dozens of targets (SMAD7/TGFBR2/CDKN1A/CCND2/…); miR-18a→SMAD2 (0.99), miR-130b→TP63. **MECHANISM TESTED (`confound_mechanism` → `prolif_confound_mechanism.tsv`) — NOT CN, it is HOST-GENE transcriptional (corrects an earlier "CN-localizable" assertion):** the source arm miR-93/106b/25 is INTRONIC in **MCM7** (a replication-licensing proliferation gene), so it is co-transcribed from the host promoter — conditioning on host MCM7 removes **65–97%** of the confound vs the locus CN only **8–10%** (miR-93 host-frac 0.72 / CN-frac 0.08). So the confound is intragenic-host co-transcription (**design axis BP**), and the correct handle is the **host mRNA, not the CN instrument** (which barely moves it — first stage ρ_abund,CN≈0.25, ρ_CN,prolif≈0.15). Sign-disagreeing arms (miR-15a/424 + vs miR-195/497 −) EXPLAIN the fragile verdicts (dose-delivery §8 attribution, NOT a verdict-subdivision — β is family-level). **⚠ abs()→−ρ fix (2026-07-11, user-caught via the host-gene lens):** the OOF coupling used \|ρ\|, which conflated repression-strengthening with sign-FLIPS to anti-repression; now repression-directed −ρ. **All three outputs (`prolif_verdict{,_genomewide,_edges}.tsv`) REGENERATED under −ρ (headline robust — GW over_control 389 vs confound 170 still ~2.3:1; the regen also adopted the live `_arm_name_map` universe correction, GW edges 1191→1242).** Docs: registry MH-100, memory `proliferation-confound-vs-mechanism`, CHANNEL_FUSION §9 AL / §6 N, ESTIMATOR_MAP row 13, forward board §A. |

Wired into `priors.{inclusion_prior,edge_priors}` as an EXPRESSION-GATED π channel (`spec_gain`, default 0=off; gate=soft log2(11) floor). Caveats: tail-driven (median Δ −0.003, concentrated in many-regulator genes MET/E2F2/CCL5/MMPs), collinearity hypothesis FAILS (interaction −0.074) ⇒ mechanism = regulator-COUNT not cooperative-families; dense (no selection) ⇒ better coupling estimator, not a lasso drop-in. **`attribution_eb.py` (MH-82):** learned-τ² posterior vs bagged NNLS for attribution — reproducibility EB 0.996 ≥ NNLS 0.983, identified set |z|>2 recovers curated drivers AND reproduces §9 identifiability (declines ESR1 221/222, ZEB1 miR-200); caveat = raw EB mean biased up for un-informed edges (half-normal prior mean). **Bayesian lasso tried (`_gibbs_blasso`, Laplace + learned λ², M≥0):** collapses to the ridge on ALL three jobs (coupling −0.363≈−0.362; attribution reproducibility 0.996, same |z|>2 recovery; does NOT fix the un-informed-edge mean bias) — because the posterior MEAN of a Laplace prior is dense (sparsity only in the MAP); genuine Bayesian selection needs the spike-and-slab, which loses on coupling. ⇒ ONE unified learned-τ² NN model (ridge; blasso equivalent): aggregate→coupling, mean±sd→attribution, |z|>2→§9. **Spike-slab-LASSO (`_gibbs_ss_blasso`, inclusion π + Laplace slab + learned λ², M≥0) tried for BOTH coupling+attribution (user):** coupling WORSE (−0.312 ≈ lasso, < dense ridge −0.362 — inclusion→sparse discards diffuse signal, confirms selection≠best-coupling); BUT attribution PIP>0.5 is the BEST co-driver-identifiability signal (ZEB1 2/2, CDKN1A 2/2 vs dense-ridge |z|>2 0/2,0/2 — evidence-π rescues collinear evidence-supported drivers). One-model fusion pending full-scale attribution check. `output/learned/shrinkage_compare.tsv`. | step | cold cost | reason |
|------|-----------|--------|
| `build_edges` (`--force-rebuild`) | ~30 s | vectorized; full-universe miRTarBase normalize |

> completes in a few minutes.

single-driver 88.8%, co-drivers 71.1%)** — because they are the SAME KIND of estimator (both variation/coupling-based). **Un-collapsing buys NOTHING for attribution: the collapsed model was already doing this, and neither measures the quantity within-family attribution IS.** **(4) POSITIVE CONTROLS (the docs' own canonical deliverers) — the dose lens recovers them, the coupling lenses do not:** **CCND1←miR-16** (Ring-1): cn_copy ✓ / abundance ✓ / chimera ✓ · collapsed driver **miR-424** ✗ · arm β **miR-15b** ✗. **ZEB1←miR-200c** (Ring-1): cn_copy ✓ / abundance ✓ / chimera ✓ · collapsed driver **miR-429** ✗ · arm β ✓. **CCND1/miR-17~92:** chimera+dose = miR-17-5p; | ⭐⭐ THE EDGE-LEVEL NULL IS MISCALIBRATED — empirical site-free null (Efron) recalibrates every per-edge FDR count in the subproject | 2026-07-13 | ok | ~5 min | **MH-123. THE THEORETICAL t-NULL IS WRONG BY 3–4× IN SCALE. Under an HONEST null, per-edge miRNA→target discovery in TCGA bulk is essentially IMPOSSIBLE at n=1041; the real signal is a SET-LEVEL distributional shift, not per-edge significance.** **THE DIAGNOSIS.** Build a SITE-FREE class: (arm,gene) pairs with NO TargetScan context++ site AND no scanMiR duplex (repression > −0.5) — pairs that CANNOT repress by any modelled mechanism, but carry the SAME population structure. **Measured (TCGA, n=1041, 60,000 site-free pairs): under the theoretical t-null, 35.3% of them are "FDR-significant" (q<0.05, ρ<0) under core C and 25.0% under the composition-adjusted C.** A 25–35% false-positive rate at a nominal 5% FDR. **THE FITTED NULL (per arm-abundance quintile, robust median / 1.4826·MAD):** theoretical would be μ=0, **sd=0.0311**; MEASURED site-free null is **μ₀ = −0.048…−0.021, sd₀ = 0.105…0.132 (core C)** and **μ₀ = −0.027…−0.011, sd₀ = 0.083…0.097 (composition-adjusted)**. ⇒ **the real null is 3–4× WIDER than the t-distribution assumes AND its mean is NEGATIVE** (a global miRNA↔mRNA anti-correlation from shared population structure). **Composition adjustment shrinks both the shift and the width but does NOT close the gap** — so the confound is only partly compartmental (proliferation/state/global programs carry the rest). **RECALIBRATED COUNTS (composition-adjusted C, Efron empirical null, BH q<0.05):** HE **1,313 (31.3%) → 1 (0.0%)** · ORPHAN 3,891 (83.5%) → 200 (4.3%) · site-free 14,971 (25.0%) → 0 (calibrated by construction). **⚠ FIRST IMPLEMENTATION WAS BROKEN AND THE GIVEAWAY WAS HE→0: counting EXCEEDANCES against a 12k-per-bin null is RESOLUTION-LIMITED (min p = 1/N ≈ 8e-5), so BH across 4,194 tests (needing p<1.2e-5) can NEVER fire. Fixed with the Efron form — FIT the null's location+scale, score against N(μ₀,σ₀), continuous p.** **⭐ THE RECONCILIATION (both are true):** HE edges ARE genuinely enriched — **abundance-matched (log2RPM>4) vs the site-free class: OR 1.41, Fisher p=2.5e-13, and it SURVIVES composition adjustment (OR 1.43 core → 1.41 deconv, essentially unchanged ⇒ THE CURATED SIGNAL WAS NEVER COMPARTMENT ARITHMETIC)** — but a typical HE edge has ρ≈−0.15 against σ₀=0.09 ⇒ z≈−1.4, p≈0.08 ⇒ **real but nowhere near surviving FDR across 4,000 tests.** ⇒ **THE HE SIGNAL IS A DISTRIBUTIONAL SHIFT, NOT PER-EDGE SIGNIFICANCE.** The ORPHAN's 200 surviving "hits" are the CIRCULAR SELECTION showing through (those edges were nominated FOR extreme ρ). **⭐⭐ SEQUENCE CANDIDACY IS MOSTLY COMPARTMENT ARITHMETIC (the user's question — "isn't deconv supposed to take care of it?"): abundance-matched (log2RPM>4), predicted-site (CAND) vs site-free (FAKE): core C OR=1.36 (p=7e-24) → composition-adjusted OR=1.11 (p=8e-04). MEANWHILE HE vs FAKE is UNCHANGED (1.43→1.41). And HE vs CAND goes from n.s. (OR 1.05, p=0.12) to SIGNIFICANT (OR 1.27, p=5e-08).** ⇒ **composition adjustment does not wash everything out — it SEPARATES: curation survives it intact, sequence prediction does not.** **CONSEQUENCE: every per-edge FDR count in this subproject (incl. MH-114's survival grid, `discovery`'s in_partial ρ<−0.15 gate, `dossier`'s `couples`) is inflated by a miscalibrated null.** The defensible readouts are (a) SET-LEVEL enrichment vs an abundance-matched site-free null and (b) AGGREGATE/gene-level PREDICTION (which does not depend on the per-edge null at all). **RESERVE "discovery" LANGUAGE FOR THE AGGREGATE LANE.** **⚠ HONEST COUNTER-CAVEAT: the site-free class certainly contains SOME real non-canonical targets, which widens the null and makes it CONSERVATIVE. The truth is between the theoretical null (clearly far too liberal) and this one — but a 3–4× scale error is not a small correction.** Outputs: scratchpad `empnull.py` / `hf2.py`; wire the site-free null into `discovery`/`dossier` as the calibration standard. |

Existence vs importance, arm vs family, S+L** | 2026-07-13 | ok | ~3 h | **THE ANSWER: THE EDGES ARE REAL; THE WEIGHTS ARE NOT TRUSTWORTHY AS ATTRIBUTION.** **(1) EXISTENCE ✅ three independent ways** — set-level vs abundance-matched site-free (**OR 1.41, p=2.5e-13**, survives composition); **decoy-controlled model test** (strict decoys: no seed-family mate, no genomic-cluster mate, \|r\|<0.30 vs every real regulator, **abundance-matched**, matched prior — REAL PIP>0.5 **21.0% vs 14.4%, p=8.9e-05**; prediction field **+0.146 vs +0.104, p=2.1e-07**) — **but decoys reach 71% of the real field and absorb ~36–49% of \|β\|** ⇒ NOT just abundance, but a **small margin on a large background**; and the **exogenous CN instrument**, exclusion-gated + abundance-matched (**arm: π_causal −0.0235 vs −0.0075, p<1e-4**; **family (`exclusion` grouped by CN segment = the "aggregate loci by the arms' loci" instrument): −0.0227 vs −0.0083, p=7.7e-05, seed-stable 10/10 matching draws**). ⚠ **the RAW instrument FAILS (p=0.21) — aneuploidy makes CN→target negative for 86.5% of *impossible* edges; the exclusion gate is ESSENTIAL.** **(2) IMPORTANCE ❌** — the learned β does NOT name the literature-canonical regulator at EITHER resolution: per-arm **42.9% (p=0.38, chance)**; per-family **1/16 = 6% vs 8.1% chance**. Head-to-head on the SAME 16 genes with exact seed-family matching: β 6% · dose 19% · **CN-causal π 25% vs 21.6% chance (p=0.46)** — CN-causal leads and its hits are all canonical (ZEB1←miR-200, BCL2L11←miR-17, MET/CD44←miR-34), paired McNemar 4-vs-1 (p=0.19), **but UNDERPOWERED — the literature set (n=16), not the estimator, is the constraint. NOT a negative.** **(3) ⭐ S+L REFUTED (Q7)** — RUV factors (Gagnon-Bartsch & Speed 2012) from **negative-control arms** (leave-the-design-out, so no arm is partialled on a factor containing itself): removing the low-rank part destroys the REAL signal **faster** than the decoys, **monotonically** — separation +6.8% (k=0) → +2.5% (k=2) → +1.1% (k=5) → **+0.3% (k=10)**; decoy \|β\| share never moves (49%). The top miRNA factors ARE proliferation (r=0.33) and purity (r=0.41) — **but that is where the biology acts** ⇒ **signal and background occupy the SAME subspace; no endogenous re-basis can identify these edges. Identification must be EXOGENOUS — which is exactly why the CN instrument works (axiom 4).** **(4) ⛔ CORRECTNESS BUG FOUND+FIXED+VERIFIED:** `spike_slab._rtnorm_pos` violated its own **β≥0** support — for `mu/s < −7.0345` `ndtr` saturates to 1.0, `u` clips, and the draw becomes the **deterministic constant** `mu+7.0345·s` < 0 (100% negative, 1 unique value in 200 draws); **3.15%** of persisted `readouts_edges.tsv` β were impossible. | `docs/MH126_ANEUPLOIDY_AND_GRADED_ATTRIBUTION.md` — **MH-126: (1) aneuploidy in the CN instrument · (2) what the learned model is FOR · (3) the GRADED attribution question** (scratchpad only; no repo module changed) | 2026-07-13 | ok (⛔ downgrades MH-124's CN existence leg) | ~4 h (Q1 main run 294 s / 8 workers · Q3 build+tests ~2 min · circularity gate 96 s · 3 adversarial lenses) | **THREE ANSWERS, TWO OF THEM PARTLY NEGATIVE.** **(1) ANEUPLOIDY = A CALIBRATION FIX, NOT A POWER FIX.** A derived per-sample **ploidy** term (mean ASCAT3 CN over the 506 miRNA hairpin loci) added to the instrument's C drops the fraction of **IMPOSSIBLE (site-free decoy)** edges with a NEGATIVE CN→target reduced form from **86.4% → 50.0% (exactly the null)**; HE 89.3% → 53.6%; exclusion-clean HE 34.6% → **55.6%**; first stage STRENGTHENS (mean ρ +0.096 → +0.118) ⇒ it removes nuisance, not signal. **The MECHANISM is NOT the co-amplification story that motivated the request** (that predicts a POSITIVE reduced form): it is a **ploidy SIGN FLIP** — corr(resid(CN_locus\|C), ploidy) = **+0.437** (100% positive) while corr(resid(Y\|C), ploidy) = **−0.135** (negative in **94.6% of 482 genes**, because `target_cn` is already in C and TPM dilutes) ⇒ product is negative for ANY pair, site or no site; **ONE per-gene scalar explains R²=0.554** of the reduced form (Spearman +0.728, p=2e-268). The **official** aneuploidy measures barely help (Taylor AS alone 81.5%, FGA alone 82.9%); the derived ploidy does it all alone (49.9%). **(2) ⛔ BUT THE ADVERSARIAL VERIFIERS REFUTED THE POWER STORY AND ONE LEG OF MH-124.** **`pi_causal` IS NOT EXOGENOUS** — `instrument.py` computes it as **γ_s · b_fam**, first stage × the **OLS partial slope of Y on X_fam given [C, Z_s]** = exactly the instrument-ORTHOGONAL (endogenous) variation (identity re-verified by re-running `INS.exclusion`; `b_fam` ≈ the plain no-CN partial correlation, ρ≈0.93; the whole "3× separation" was **reproduced from a simulation with ZERO causal effect**). The **only** exogenous factor, **γ_s, is SITE-BLIND: HE +0.19931 vs DECOY +0.19844, ratio 1.004×, p=0.20.** And the one genuinely exogenous statistic (the reduced form) shows **NO HE-vs-decoy effect** once the unit is the **LOCUS** (τ=−0.0015, **p=0.67**) or once arms are matched on their own site-free background (p=0.038 → **p=0.71**). **UNIT-OF-ANALYSIS:** 3,111 edges = only **482 genes / 409 arms / 277 loci** (ICC gene 0.372 / arm 0.352 / locus 0.300); under **gene+arm two-way FE, 59–72% of the claimed gap is composition** and the "raw instrument RESCUED" claim **dies (p 0.038 → 0.70)**. **The fix does NOT improve discrimination (ΔAUC = −0.002, n.s.)**, and **attribution is UNCHANGED: literature family named 4/15 (p=0.20) → 4/16 (p=0.18)** — **UNDERPOWERED (the n=16 literature set is the binding constraint), NOT a negative.** ⚠ **SIDE-EFFECT: after the fix the exclusion GATE stops discriminating** (decoy 57.7% "clean" vs HE 55.6%) — a contract change for every consumer that uses it as a filter. **(3) WHAT LEARNING IS FOR (registry-verified, NOT re-derived here):** de-confounding (β given C) + joint apportionment + **out-of-sample transfer** — MH-115: only the learned β predicts CPTAC protein (**−0.0364, p=5.3e-13**; composition-adjusted **−0.0126, p=9.6e-04**) where abundance (p=0.20) and the pressure heuristic (p=0.11) are nothing; edge level **β −0.278 (p=2e-50)**, **partial β\|abundance −0.140 (p=1.5e-13)** while **abundance\|β is redundant (p=0.52)**; MH-116/117: ~85% retained OOF over patients, and β transports to the **ORPHAN** (uncurated) universe where the heuristic cannot even be formed. **What it is NOT: an attribution engine** — an IDENTIFIABILITY ceiling (median posterior SD/\|β\| **0.799**; only **27.8% of 5,117 units identified**), not a fitting failure. **(4) THE GRADED QUESTION — YES, WEAKLY, AND NOT ROBUSTLY.** **CIRCULARITY GATE PASSES HARD:** β is **bit-identical** (max\|Δ\| = **0.0**, 0/48 genes moved) under within-gene-SHUFFLED w, CONSTANT w, **and** a shuffle at w's SOURCE (`ledger.edge_weights`), while `prior_pi`/`pip_discovery` move in 41–42/48 ⇒ `wf` is a dead variable and the dense Gibbs runs at π≡1 ⇒ the test is clean (re-verified end-to-end through `readouts.gene_readouts` by the adversary). **TEST 1 (n=32 literature genes, DOUBLE MH-124's 16):** normalized rank of the canonical family (chance 0.5) — **β 0.518 (p=0.66) = EXACTLY CHANCE** (top-1 1/32 vs 9.4% chance); shapley 0.370 (p=0.018); **ABUNDANCE 0.240 (p<1e-4) BEATS BOTH** (paired abundance−β −0.278, p=5e-04). Cluster-bootstrap over the **13 distinct canonical families** (the real unit): abundance CI [0.126, 0.356] p=0.0000, shapley p=0.023, β still chance ⇒ **leverage-proof.** **TEST 3 (3,797 gene×family rows, 524 genes):** per-gene Spearman(β, w) = **+0.0683 (p=4.2e-03; within-gene permutation p=0.005)** — real but **small**; and under the adversary's **family-preserving null** (permute w within family across its genes) β **survives at p=0.010** (family "fame" = 27% of it) while **shapley DIES (p=0.297 — 85% is fame)**. ⚠ **Under a FULL FAMILY fixed effect the author's "✅ SURVIVES (b=+0.0506, p=9.8e-03)" becomes b≈+0.031, p=0.074–0.097 (BORDERLINE)** and the **CN π's "strongest gradient" claim DIES (p=0.121)**. **Study-bias channel measured: Spearman(abundance, w) = +0.1710 — but Spearman(β, abundance) within gene = −0.0801 ⇒ abundance SUPPRESSES rather than confounds β's gradient ("canonical = abundant" is FALSE for β).** **NEXT (§4 of the doc):** persist ploidy for CALIBRATION only, with **LOO-ploidy + a VIF check** first, then trace the ripple (`exclusion.tsv`, `cn_edge_validation.tsv`, `cn_family_*.tsv`, `channel_cn` all go stale); **stop calling `pi_causal` exogenous**; fix the unit of analysis (gene+arm FE / locus clusters) in every HE-vs-decoy test; and **SCALE THE LITERATURE SET** — it, not the instrument, is what gates attribution. Scripts: scratchpad `common.py`/`characterise.py`/`run_main.py`/`analyse.py`/`lit.py`/`sens.py` · `w_circularity.py` · `mh126_{build,lit,tests}.py` · `adv_*`/`circ_*`/`pow*`. **No repo module was modified.** |

Deployed design = the **`in_caliper` TRIMMED** set (abundance MWU **p=0.975**, signed Δ=−0.003). **⭐⭐ THE VERDICT — IN-COHORT, THE LEARNING IS NOT MORE THAN FITTING.** TCGA 5-fold OOF (n≈1041; C-coefs, z-stats and β all TRAIN-only): FAKE1 899 genes **R1 real-unfitted −0.0268 · R2 fake-unfitted −0.0157 · R3 REAL-FITTED −0.0465 · R4 FAKE-FITTED −0.0367** ⇒ the decoy **reaches 78.9%** (FAKE2 84.9%; on n_fam≥2 FAKE2 reaches **91.3%**). **R3 vs R4: Δ=−0.0098 [−0.0213,+0.0018], real wins 52.5%, Wilcoxon p=0.211** (FAKE2 p=0.577; direction stable in 5/5 seeds, never significant; **MDE=0.0161 ⇒ UNDERPOWERED, not proven zero**). **The decoy gains from FITTING as much as the real set does (R4−R2 = −0.0210, p=3.2e-30 vs R3−R1 = −0.0198, p=4.9e-18)** ⇒ fitting is a GENERIC gain. **And the fitted DECOY beats REAL UNFITTED ABUNDANCE (Δ=−0.0100, p=0.054 all genes; Δ=−0.0357, p=3.6e-04 on multi-regulator genes; FAKE2 p=0.038/5.4e-05)** ⇒ **MH-115's "β beats mere abundance" is a statement about FITTING, not curation — registry row annotated.** Structural: 64% of trimmed genes are 1-family, where `Xz@β` is a monotone rescaling of abundance ⇒ **R3≡R1 exactly** (99.5% exact ties) and no fitting happens at all. **✅ WHAT SURVIVES THE CONTROL — OUT-OF-COHORT TRANSFER.** Frozen TCGA β on CPTAC (n=101, never refit): **REAL fitted predicts CPTAC mRNA (−0.0189, p=8.9e-04, 685 genes) and the MATCHED FAKE does not (−0.0056, p=0.19)**; paired REAL-vs-FAKE **p=0.030** (FAKE1) / **0.027** (FAKE2), and under FAKE2 it **survives composition** (Δ=−0.0126, **p=0.018**). Head-to-head partial is ASYMMETRIC (REAL\|FAKE −0.0198 p=3.6e-04; FAKE\|REAL −0.0041 p=0.29). **⚠ CPTAC PROTEIN CANNOT ARBITRATE** — under this from-scratch field the **REAL** model is itself null (p=0.38 core / 0.75 comp) and the test is underpowered (MDE 0.018–0.020, power 0.56 for a ceiling-sized gap). ⚠ **DISCREPANCY TO RECONCILE:** `coupling_grid` (MH-125-fixed, re-run 18:22) still reports learned·protein·core −0.035 p=7.8e-12 over 1,162 genes — my 685-gene TRIMMED universe does not reproduce it; diff the two field constructions. **⛔ THE STRATIFIED RESCUE FAILS.** R3-vs-R4 by regulator count (1-3 p=0.599 · 4-8 p=0.173 · 9-15 p=0.508 · **26+ NOT decoy-controllable at all**, 0 eligible fake arms — hub 3′UTRs are target-rich) and by Shapley class (SINGLE_DRIVER Δ=−0.0145 p=0.273 · CO_DRIVERS −0.0362 p=0.176 · DIFFUSE −0.0320 p=0.248): **no stratum is significant, and the ordering is the OPPOSITE of the prediction** (gap smallest where credit is concentrated). An adversarial lens further showed the class **reach-% ordering is indistinguishable from shuffled labels (perm p=0.92)** ⇒ **class ordering WITHDRAWN as noise.** **⭐ THE ONE THING THAT SEPARATES IS FIT-FREE:** the top tertile of **curated evidence weight `w`** gives real > fake **in the UNFITTED rung as much as the fitted one** (R1vR2 Δ=−0.0575 p=3.3e-04 **BH q=0.0059**; R3vR4 −0.0580 q=0.0059) — but it **does NOT replicate under FAKE2 (q=0.20)** ⇒ SUGGESTIVE LEAD, not a result. **ADVERSARIAL (6 lenses):** leakage ×2 — **leak-free** (bit-exact re-implementation; a 200-permutation patient-relabel null through the pipeline centres BOTH arms at ρ≈0, SD 0.0036; permuted-Y probe is POSITIVE/conservative and arm-symmetric; max conceivable leak ≈0.007 ρ and it helps the decoy equally). Match-quality ×2 — decoys are **not proxies** (subspace R² median 0.028), abundance/detection matched, and the axes that ARE mismatched (SD, HE degree 20 vs 3, TargetScan promiscuity 504 vs 0) **all handicap the FAKE**; a size-matched decoy still reaches 76.1%. Stratum ×2 — one **REFUTED** the blanket "n.s. in every stratum" (the w-hi tertile), the other **killed the class ordering**. **Evidence:** `output/learned/mh127/{ladder_oof.tsv.gz, p2_transfer_results.tsv.gz, mh127_tcga_rungs.tsv, mh127_cptac_rungs.tsv, fake_regulator_sets.tsv.gz, gene_strata.tsv.gz, stratlens_all_tests.tsv, code/}`. |

(p=0.49–0.62). **⭐ Q3 — THE CN INSTRUMENT CANNOT BE THE ATTRIBUTION GOLD STANDARD.** Phase 3 reported β +0.736 > identity +0.453 > dose +0.093 vs `pi_causal` (exclusion-clean, F>10, C+ploidy; 137 genes / 444 families) **and its own conclusion was REFUTED by three independent adversaries.** **THE LEAK:** `pi_causal = γ_s·b_fam` and `b_fam` is the **OBSERVATIONAL** OLS slope of Y on X_fam — the same Y/X/C/samples β is fit on ⇒ **β agrees with `b_fam` (NO copy number in it at all) at +0.873, HIGHER than with `pi_causal` (+0.736)** (`mh127/bfam.log`). **THE CORRECT NULL** (Y-residual permutation, design/CN/C held 100% fixed, 25 perms, `mh127/null_ylane.tsv`): β's chance baseline vs `pi_causal` is **+0.618**, identity's is **+0.104** ⇒ on **excess over the null**, **identity (+0.349) BEATS β (+0.118)**; and on the **fully exogenous reduced form `pi_raw`** they are **TIED (identity +0.324 vs β +0.309)**, both p=0.038 (the 25-perm floor). Gating raises the mean margin only by enriching for larger effects. ⇒ **the axes predict the AVERAGE margin, never the PER-GENE SIGN — β is an aggregate bet, not per-gene actionable.** Promoted to **axiom 8** in `CLAUDE.md` with `learned/gene_axes.py` as the mandated tool. |

It was — FN1 sits at the **89th percentile** of `comp_tcga_mrna_driver_share` (0.908), 11th on `driver_ret`, 13th on `median_retention`. **I had tested gateability ONLY on the ENSEMBLE axis (`reg_dose_hhi`), found harm flat, and over-concluded.** The composition axis separates the tails at **p=0.00999** (ensemble: p=0.10). **Gates vs a 34.7% / 58.5% baseline: ensemble-only 33.9% / 63.8% (harm unmoved) · composition-only 32.5% / 63.7% · BOTH → 27.6% / 69.3%, mean Δ −0.0468, on 19% of genes** — retaining 112/163 strong-help genes and **none** of MYC/CCND1/HIF1A/FOXC1/TXNIP/EGFR lost. ⇒ **COMPOSITION axes predict the SIGN, ENSEMBLE axes predict the MAGNITUDE; β IS per-gene actionable behind a two-family gate.** Axiom 8 rewritten accordingly. |

Gate ladder: **nominal hurt 34.7 → 27.6 → 21.6 → 18.8% while GENUINE harm stays pinned at 6.8 / 6.3 / 6.8 / 6.2%**; mean Δ improves 3.7× to −0.0761 on 7% of genes. ⇒ **gates buy noise-removal + magnitude-enrichment, NOT damage-avoidance.** Irreducible eight: MET, SMAD7, IL6, CDKN1A, ETS1, BCL2L2, XIAP, CDK4. ⚠ 8-vs-119 needs d≈1.05 for 80% power, so that contrast's null is uninformative; the continuous scan is the load-bearing statement.
